#### `fromShape`
*function*

fromShape(Shape): Returns a Path object from a Part Shape (deprecated - use fromShapes() instead)

#### `fromShapes`
*function*

fromShapes(shapes, start=Vector(), return_end=False arc_plane=1, sort_mode=1, min_dist=0.0, abscissa=3.0, nearest_k=3, orientation=0, direction=0, threshold=0.0, retract_axis=2, retraction=0.0, resume_height=0.0, segmentation=0.0, feedrate=0.0, feedrate_v=0.0, verbose=true, abs_center=false, preamble=true, deflection=0.01)

Returns a Path object from a list of shapes

* shapes: input list of shapes.

* start (Vector()): feed start position, and also serves as a hint of path entry.

* return_end (False): if True, returns tuple (path, endPosition).

* arc_plane(1): 0=None,1=Auto,2=XY,3=ZX,4=YZ,5=Variable. Arc drawing plane, corresponding to G17, G18, and G19.
If not 'None', the output wires will be transformed to align with the selected plane,
and the corresponding GCode will be inserted.
'Auto' means the plane is determined by the first encountered arc plane. If the found
plane does not align to any GCode plane, XY plane is used.
'Variable' means the arc plane can be changed during operation to align to the
arc encountered.

* sort_mode(1): 0=None,1=2D5,2=3D,3=Greedy. Wire sorting mode to optimize travel distance.
'2D5' explode shapes into wires, and groups the shapes by its plane. The 'start' position
chooses the first plane to start. The algorithm will then sort within the plane and then
move on to the next nearest plane.
'3D' makes no assumption of planarity. The sorting is done across 3D space.
'Greedy' like '2D5' but will try to minimize travel by searching for nearest path below
the current milling layer. The path in lower layer is only selected if the moving distance
is within the value given in 'threshold'.

* min_dist(0.0): minimum distance for the generated new wires. Wires maybe broken if the
algorithm see fits. Set to zero to disable wire breaking.

* abscissa(3.0): Controls vertex sampling on wire for nearest point searching
The sampling is dong using OCC GCPnts_UniformAbscissa

* nearest_k(3): Nearest k sampling vertices are considered during sorting

* orientation(0): 0=Normal,1=Reversed. Enforce loop orientation
'Normal' means CCW for outer wires when looking against the positive axis direction, 
and CW for inner wires. 'Reversed' means the other way round

* direction(0): 0=None,1=XPositive,2=XNegative,3=YPositive,4=YNegative,5=ZPositive,6=ZNegative. Enforce open path direction

* threshold(0.0): If two wire's end points are separated within this threshold, they are consider
as connected. You may want to set this to the tool diameter to keep the tool down.

* retract_axis(2): 0=X,1=Y,2=Z. Tool retraction axis

* retraction(0.0): Tool retraction absolute coordinate along retraction axis

* resume_height(0.0): When return from last retraction, this gives the pause height relative to the Z
value of the next move.

* segmentation(0.0): Break long curves into segments of this length. One use case is for PCB autolevel,
so that more correction points can be inserted

* feedrate(0.0): Normal move feed rate

* feedrate_v(0.0): Vertical only (step down) move feed rate

* verbose(true): If true, each motion GCode will contain full coordinate and feedrate

* abs_center(false): Use absolute arc center mode (G90.1)

* preamble(true): Emit preambles

* deflection(0.01): Deflection for non circular curve discretization. It also also used for
discretizing circular wires when you 'Explode' the shape for wire operations

#### `read`
*function*

read(filename,[document]): Imports a GCode file into the given document

#### `show`
*function*

show(path,[string]): Add the path to the active document or create one if no document exists

#### `sortWires`
*function*

sortWires(shapes, start=Vector(),  arc_plane=1 sort_mode=1, min_dist=0.0, abscissa=3.0, nearest_k=3, orientation=0, direction=0, threshold=0.0, retract_axis=2)

Returns (wires,end), where 'wires' is sorted across Z value and with optimized travel distance,
and 'end' is the ending position of the whole wires. If arc_plane==1, it returns (wires,end,arc_plane),
where arc_plane is the found plane if any, or unchanged.

* shapes: input shape list

* start (Vector()): optional start position.

* arc_plane(1): 0=None,1=Auto,2=XY,3=ZX,4=YZ,5=Variable. Arc drawing plane, corresponding to G17, G18, and G19.
If not 'None', the output wires will be transformed to align with the selected plane,
and the corresponding GCode will be inserted.
'Auto' means the plane is determined by the first encountered arc plane. If the found
plane does not align to any GCode plane, XY plane is used.
'Variable' means the arc plane can be changed during operation to align to the
arc encountered.

* sort_mode(1): 0=None,1=2D5,2=3D,3=Greedy. Wire sorting mode to optimize travel distance.
'2D5' explode shapes into wires, and groups the shapes by its plane. The 'start' position
chooses the first plane to start. The algorithm will then sort within the plane and then
move on to the next nearest plane.
'3D' makes no assumption of planarity. The sorting is done across 3D space.
'Greedy' like '2D5' but will try to minimize travel by searching for nearest path below
the current milling layer. The path in lower layer is only selected if the moving distance
is within the value given in 'threshold'.

* min_dist(0.0): minimum distance for the generated new wires. Wires maybe broken if the
algorithm see fits. Set to zero to disable wire breaking.

* abscissa(3.0): Controls vertex sampling on wire for nearest point searching
The sampling is dong using OCC GCPnts_UniformAbscissa

* nearest_k(3): Nearest k sampling vertices are considered during sorting

* orientation(0): 0=Normal,1=Reversed. Enforce loop orientation
'Normal' means CCW for outer wires when looking against the positive axis direction, 
and CW for inner wires. 'Reversed' means the other way round

* direction(0): 0=None,1=XPositive,2=XNegative,3=YPositive,4=YNegative,5=ZPositive,6=ZNegative. Enforce open path direction

* threshold(0.0): If two wire's end points are separated within this threshold, they are consider
as connected. You may want to set this to the tool diameter to keep the tool down.

* retract_axis(2): 0=X,1=Y,2=Z. Tool retraction axis

#### `FemMesh.writeABAQUS`
*method*

Write out as ABAQUS inp
                    writeABAQUS(file, int elemParam, bool groupParam, str volVariant, str faceVariant, str edgeVariant)

                    elemParam:
                        0: All elements
                        1: Highest elements only
                        2: FEM elements only (only edges not belonging to faces and faces not belonging to volumes)

                    groupParam:
                        True: Write group data
                        False: Do not write group data

                    volVariant: Volume elements
                        "standard": Tetra4 -> C3D4, Penta6 -> C3D6, Hexa8 -> C3D8, Tetra10 -> C3D10, Penta15 -> C3D15, Hexa20 -> C3D20
                        "reduced": Hexa8 -> C3D8R, Hexa20 -> C3D20R
                        "incompatible": Hexa8 -> C3D8I
                        "modified": Tetra10 -> C3D10T
                        "fluid": Tetra4 -> F3D4, Penta6 -> F3D6, Hexa8  -> F3D8

                    faceVariant: Face elements
                        "shell": Tria3 -> S3, Quad4 -> S4, Tria6 -> S6, Quad8 -> S8
                        "shell reduced": Tria3 -> S3, Quad4 -> S4R, Tria6 -> S6, Quad8 -> S8R
                        "membrane": Tria3 -> M3D3, Quad4 -> M3D4, Tria6 -> M3D6, Quad8 -> M3D8
                        "membrane reduced": Tria3 -> M3D3, Quad4 -> M3D4R, Tria6 -> M3D6, Quad8 -> M3D8R
                        "stress": Tria3 -> CPS3, Quad4 -> CPS4, Tria6 -> CPS6, Quad8 -> CPS8
                        "stress reduced": Tria3 -> CPS3, Quad4 -> CPS4R, Tria6 -> CPS6, Quad8 -> CPS8R
                        "strain": Tria3 -> CPE3, Quad4 -> CPE4, Tria6 -> CPE6, Quad8 -> CPE8
                        "strain reduced": Tria3 -> CPE3, Quad4 -> CPE4R, Tria6 -> CPE6, Quad8 -> CPE8R
                        "axisymmetric": Tria3 -> CAX3, Quad4 -> CAX4, Tria6 -> CAX6, Quad8 -> CAX8
                        "axisymmetric reduced": Tria3 -> CAX3, Quad4 -> CAX4R, Tria6 -> CAX6, Quad8 -> CAX8R

                    edgeVariant: Edge elements
                        "beam": Seg2 -> B31, Seg3 -> B32
                        "beam reduced": Seg2 -> B31R, Seg3 -> B32R
                        "truss": Seg2 -> T3D2, eg3 -> T3D3
                        "network": Seg3 -> D

                    Elements are selected according to CalculiX availability.
                    For example if volume variant "modified" is selected, Tetra10 mesh
                    elements are assigned to C3D10T and remain elements uses "standard".
                    Axisymmetric, plane strain and plane stress elements expect nodes in the plane z=0.

### Module `ReverseEngineering`

#### `approxCurve`
*function*

Approximate curve

#### `approxSurface`
*function*

approxSurface(Points, UDegree=3, VDegree=3, NbUPoles=6, NbVPoles=6,
Smooth=True, Weight=0.1, Grad=1.0, Bend=0.0, Curv=0.0
Iterations=5, Correction=True, PatchFactor=1.0, UVDirs=((ux, uy, uz), (vx, vy, vz)))

Points: the input data (e.g. a point cloud or mesh)
UDegree: the degree in u parametric direction
VDegree: the degree in v parametric direction
NbUPoles: the number of control points in u parametric direction
NbVPoles: the number of control points in v parametric direction
Smooth: use energy terms to create a smooth surface
Weight: weight of the energy terms altogether
Grad: weight of the gradient term
Bend: weight of the bending energy term
Curv: weight of the deviation of curvature term
Iterations: number of iterations
Correction: perform a parameter correction of each iteration step
PatchFactor: create an extended surface
UVDirs: set the u,v parameter directions as tuple of two vectors
        If not set then they will be determined by computing a best-fit plane

#### `normalEstimation`
*function*

normalEstimation(Points,[KSearch=0, SearchRadius=0]) -> Normals
KSearch is an int and used to search the k-nearest neighbours in
the k-d tree. Alternatively, SearchRadius (a float) can be used
as spatial distance to determine the neighbours of a point
Example:

import ReverseEngineering as Reen
pts=App.ActiveDocument.ActiveObject.Points
nor=Reen.normalEstimation(pts,KSearch=5)

f=App.ActiveDocument.addObject('Points::FeaturePython','Normals')
f.addProperty('Points::PropertyNormalList','Normal')
f.Points=pts
f.Normal=nor
f.ViewObject.Proxy=0
f.ViewObject.DisplayMode=1

### Module `Show`

#### `TempoVis(document, stack=<class 'Show.mTempoVis.MAINSTACK'>, **kwargs)`
*class*

TempoVis - helper object to save visibilities of objects before doing
some GUI editing, hiding or showing relevant stuff during edit, and
then restoring all visibilities after editing.

Constructors:
TempoVis(document, stack = MAINSTACK, **kwargs): creates a new TempoVis.

document: required. Objects not belonging to the document can't be modified via TempoVis.

stack: optional. Which stack to insert this new TV into. Can be:
a TVStack instance (then, the new TV is added to the top of the stack),
MAINSTACK special value (a global stack for the document will be used), or
None (then, the TV is not in any stack, and can be manually instertd into one if desired).

Any additional keyword args are assigned as attributes. You can use it to immediately set a tag, for example.

#### `TempoVis.document`
*property*

#### `TempoVis.stack`
*property*

#### `TempoVis.data`
*property*

#### `TempoVis.data_requested`
*property*

#### `TempoVis.state`
*property*

int([x]) -> integer
int(x, base=10) -> integer

Convert a number or string to an integer, or return 0 if no arguments
are given.  If x is a number, return x.__int__().  For floating point
numbers, this truncates towards zero.

If x is not a number or if base is given, then x must be a string,
bytes, or bytearray instance representing an integer literal in the
given base.  The literal can be preceded by '+' or '-' and be surrounded
by whitespace.  The base defaults to 10.  Valid bases are 0 and 2-36.
Base 0 means to interpret the base from the string as an integer literal.
>>> int('0b100', base=0)
4

#### `TempoVis.tag`
*property*

str(object='') -> str
str(bytes_or_buffer[, encoding[, errors]]) -> str

Create a new string object from the given object. If encoding or
errors is specified, then the object must expose a data buffer
that will be decoded using the given encoding and error handler.
Otherwise, returns the result of object.__str__() (if defined)
or repr(object).
encoding defaults to sys.getdefaultencoding().
errors defaults to 'strict'.

#### `TempoVis.has`
*method*

has(self, detail): returns True if this TV has this detail value saved.
        example: tv.has(VProperty(obj, "Visibility"))

#### `TempoVis.stored_val`
*method*

stored_val(self, detail): returns value of detail remembered by this TV. If not, raises KeyError.

#### `TempoVis.save`
*method*

save(detail, mild_restore = False):saves the scene detail to be restored.
        The detail is saved only once; repeated calls are ignored.
        mild_restore: internal, do not use.

#### `TempoVis.modify`
*method*

modify(detail, mild_restore = True): modifies scene detail through this TV.
        The value is provided as an instance of SceneDetail implementation.
        The procedure takes care to account for the stack - that is, if in a TV applied
        later than this one this detail was changed too, the value saved therein is altered,
        rather than applied to the scene.

        mild_restore: if True, when restoring later, checks if the value was changed
        by user after last call to modify(), and doesn't restore if it was changed.

        Example: tv.modify(VProperty(obj, "Visibility", True))

#### `TempoVis.restoreDetail`
*method*

restoreDetail(detail, ultimate = False): restores a specific scene detail.
        ultimate: if true, the saved value is cleaned out.
        If the detail is not found, nothing is done.

#### `TempoVis.forgetDetail`
*method*

forgetDetail(detail): ditches a saved detail value, making the change done through this TV permanent.

#### `TempoVis.forget`
*method*

forget(self): clears this TV, making all changes done through it permanent.
        Also, withdraws the TV from the stack.

#### `TempoVis.restore`
*method*

restore(ultimate = True): undoes all changes done through this tempovis / restores saved scene details.
        ultimate: if true, the saved values are cleaned out, and the TV is withdrawn from
        the stack. If false, the TV will still remember stuff, and restore can be called again.

#### `TempoVis.is_in_stack`
*property*

#### `TempoVis.modifyVPProperty`
*method*

modifyVPProperty(doc_obj_or_list, prop_names, new_value = JUST_SAVE, mild_restore = None): modifies
        prop_name property of ViewProvider of doc_obj_or_list, and remembers
        original value of the property. Original values will be restored upon
        TempoVis deletion, or call to restore().

        mild_restore: test if user changed the value manually when restoring the TV.

#### `TempoVis.restoreVPProperty`
*method*

restoreVPProperty(doc_obj_or_list, prop_name, new_value): restores specific property changes.

#### `TempoVis.saveBodyVisibleFeature`
*method*

saveBodyVisibleFeature(self, doc_obj_or_list): saves Visibility of currently
        visible feature, for every body of PartDesign features in the provided list.

#### `TempoVis.show`
*method*

show(doc_obj_or_list, links_too = True): shows objects (sets their Visibility to True).
        doc_obj_or_list can be a document object, or a list of document objects.
        If links_too is True, all Links of the objects are also hidden, by setting LinkVisibility attribute of each object.

#### `TempoVis.hide`
*method*

hide(doc_obj_or_list): hides objects (sets their Visibility to False). doc_obj_or_list can be a document object, or a list of document objects

#### `TempoVis.get_all_dependent`
*method*

get_all_dependent(doc_obj, subname = None): gets all objects that depend on doc_obj. Containers and Links (if subname) required for visibility of the object are excluded from the list.

#### `TempoVis.hide_all_dependent`
*method*

hide_all_dependent(doc_obj): hides all objects that depend on doc_obj. Groups, Parts and Bodies are not hidden by this.

#### `TempoVis.show_all_dependent`
*method*

show_all_dependent(doc_obj): shows all objects that depend on doc_obj. This method is probably useless.

#### `TempoVis.restore_all_dependent`
*method*

show_all_dependent(doc_obj): restores original visibilities of all dependent objects.

#### `TempoVis.hide_all_dependencies`
*method*

hide_all_dependencies(doc_obj): hides all objects that doc_obj depends on (directly and indirectly).

#### `TempoVis.show_all_dependencies`
*method*

show_all_dependencies(doc_obj): shows all objects that doc_obj depends on (directly and indirectly). This method is probably useless.

#### `TempoVis.saveCamera`
*method*

#### `TempoVis.restoreCamera`
*method*

#### `TempoVis.setUnpickable`
*method*

setUnpickable(doc_obj_or_list, actual_pick_style = 2): sets object unpickable (transparent to clicks).
        doc_obj_or_list: object or list of objects to alter (App)
        actual_pick_style: optional parameter, specifying the actual pick style:
        0 = regular, 1 = bounding box, 2 (default) = unpickable.

        Implementation detail: uses SoPickStyle node. If viewprovider already has a node
        of this type as direct child, one is used. Otherwise, new one is created and
        inserted as the very first node, and remains there even after restore()/deleting
        tempovis.

#### `TempoVis.clipPlane`
*method*

clipPlane(doc_obj_or_list, enable, placement, offset): slices off the object with a clipping plane.
        doc_obj_or_list: object or list of objects to alter (App)
        enable: True if you want clipping, False if you want to remove clipping:
        placement: XY plane of local coordinates of the placement is the clipping plane. The placement must be in document's global coordinate system.
        offset: shifts the plane. Positive offset reveals more of the object.

        Implementation detail: uses SoClipPlane node. If viewprovider already has a node
        of this type as direct child, one is used. Otherwise, new one is created and
        inserted as the very first node. The node is left, but disabled when tempovis is restoring.

#### `TempoVis.allVisibleObjects`
*method*

allVisibleObjects(aroundObject): returns list of objects that have to be toggled invisible for only aroundObject to remain.
        If a whole container can be made invisible, it is returned, instead of its child objects.

#### `TempoVis.sketchClipPlane`
*method*

sketchClipPlane(sketch, enable = None): Clips all objects by plane of sketch.
        If enable argument is omitted, calling the routine repeatedly will toggle clipping plane.

#### `TempoVis.activateWorkbench`
*method*

#### `TempoVis.dumps`
*method*

#### `TempoVis.loads`
*method*

### Module `BOPTools`

#### `addCommands()`
*function*

addCommands(): add all GUI commands of BOPTools package to FreeCAD command manager.

#### `importAll()`
*function*

importAll(): imports all modules of BOPTools package

#### `reloadAll()`
*function*

reloadAll(): reloads all modules of BOPTools package. Useful for debugging.

- `LinkCopyOnChange` (App::PropertyEnumeration,  Link): Disabled: disable copy on change
Enabled: enable copy linked object on change of any of its properties marked as CopyOnChange
Owned: indicate the linked object has been copied and is own owned by the link. And the
       the link will try to sync any change of the original linked object back to the copy.
- `LinkCopyOnChangeGroup` (App::PropertyLink,  Link): Linked to a internal group object for holding on change copies
- `LinkCopyOnChangeSource` (App::PropertyXLink,  Link): The copy on change source object
- `LinkCopyOnChangeTouched` (App::PropertyBool,  Link): Indicating the copy on change source object has been changed
- `LinkExecute` (App::PropertyString,  Link): Link execute function. Default to 'appLinkExecute'. 'None' to disable.
- `LinkPlacement` (App::PropertyPlacement,  Link): Link placement
- `LinkTransform` (App::PropertyBool,  Link): Set to false to override linked object's placement
- `LinkedObject` (App::PropertyXLink,  Link): Linked object
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement,  Link): Alias to LinkPlacement to make the link object compatibale with other objects
- `PlacementList` (App::PropertyPlacementList,  Link): The placement for each link element
- `Scale` (App::PropertyFloat,  Link): Scale factor
- `ScaleList` (App::PropertyVectorList,  Link): The scale factors for each link element
- `ScaleVector` (App::PropertyVector,  Link): Scale factors
- `ShowElement` (App::PropertyBool,  Link): Enable link element list
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `VisibilityList` (App::PropertyBoolList,  Link): The visibility state of each link element
- `_ChildCache` (App::PropertyLinkList,  Link)
- `_LinkOwner` (App::PropertyInteger,  Link)
- `_LinkTouched` (App::PropertyBool,  Link)

- `BoundaryType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, FluidBoundary): Basic boundary type like inlet, wall, outlet,etc

- `DirectionVector` (App::PropertyVector, FluidBoundary): Direction of arrows

- `HTCoeffValue` (App::PropertyFloat, HeatTransfer): Heat transfer coefficient for convective boundary condition
- `HeatFluxValue` (App::PropertyFloat, HeatTransfer): Heat flux value for thermal boundary condition

- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)

- `Points` (App::PropertyVectorList, Constraint): Points where symbols are drawn

### `Part::Extrusion`
`doc.addObject("Part::Extrusion", "Name")`

- `Base` (App::PropertyLink, Extrude): Shape to extrude
- `Dir` (App::PropertyVector, Extrude): Direction of extrusion (also magnitude, if both lengths are zero).
- `DirLink` (App::PropertyLinkSub, Extrude): Link to edge defining extrusion direction.
- `DirMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Extrude): Sets, how Dir is updated.
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `FaceMakerClass` (App::PropertyString, Extrude): If Solid is true, this sets the facemaker class to use when converting wires to faces. Otherwise, ignored.
- `FaceMakerMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Extrude): If Solid is true, this sets the facemaker class to use when converting wires to faces. Otherwise, ignored.
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `LengthFwd` (App::PropertyDistance, Extrude): Length of extrusion along direction. If both LengthFwd and LengthRev are zero, magnitude of Dir is used.
- `LengthRev` (App::PropertyDistance, Extrude): Length of additional extrusion, against direction.
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `Reversed` (App::PropertyBool, Extrude): Set to true to swap the direction of extrusion.
- `Shape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `ShapeMaterial` (Materials::PropertyMaterial, )
- `Solid` (App::PropertyBool, Extrude): If true, extruding a wire yields a solid. If false, a shell.
- `Symmetric` (App::PropertyBool, Extrude): If true, extrusion is done in both directions to a total of LengthFwd. LengthRev is ignored.
- `TaperAngle` (App::PropertyAngle, Extrude): Sets the angle of slope (draft) to apply to the sides. The angle is for outward taper; negative value yields inward tapering.
- `TaperAngleRev` (App::PropertyAngle, Extrude): Taper angle of reverse part of extrusion.
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )

- `AttacherEngine` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Attachment): Attach engine object driving the attachment.
- `AttacherType` (App::PropertyString, Attachment): Class name of attach engine object driving the attachment.
- `AttachmentOffset` (App::PropertyPlacement, Attachment): Extra placement to apply in addition to attachment (in local coordinates)
- `AttachmentSupport` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, Attachment): Support of the 2D geometry
- `BaseFeature` (App::PropertyLink, )
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `MapMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Attachment): Mode of attachment to other object
- `MapPathParameter` (App::PropertyFloat, Attachment): Sets point of curve to map the sketch to. 0..1 = start..end
- `MapReversed` (App::PropertyBool, Attachment): Reverse Z direction (flip sketch upside down)
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `Radius1` (App::PropertyLength, Ellipsoid): Radius in local z-direction
- `Radius2` (App::PropertyLength, Ellipsoid): Radius in local x-direction
- `Radius3` (App::PropertyLength, Ellipsoid): Radius in local y-direction
If zero, it is equal to Radius2
- `Refine` (App::PropertyBool, Part Design): Refine shape (clean up redundant edges) after adding/subtracting
- `Shape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `ShapeMaterial` (Materials::PropertyMaterial, )
- `Suppressed` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Is object suppressed

- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `_Body` (App::PropertyLinkHidden, Base)

### `PartDesign::AdditiveHelix`
`doc.addObject("PartDesign::AdditiveHelix", "Name")`

- `AddSubShape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `AllowMultiFace` (App::PropertyBool, SketchBased): Allow multiple faces in profile
- `Angle` (App::PropertyAngle, Helix): The angle of the cone that forms a hull around the helix.
Non-zero values turn the helix into a conical spiral.
Positive values make the radius grow, nevatige shrink.
- `Axis` (App::PropertyVector, Helix): The helix' direction; derived from the reference axis.
- `Base` (App::PropertyVector, Helix): The center point of the helix' start; derived from the reference axis.
- `BaseFeature` (App::PropertyLink, )
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `Growth` (App::PropertyDistance, Helix): The growth of the helix' radius per turn.
Non-zero values turn the helix into a conical spiral.
- `HasBeenEdited` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): If false, the tool will propose an initial value for the pitch based on the profile bounding box,
so that self intersection is avoided.
- `Height` (App::PropertyLength, Helix): The height of the helix' path, not accounting for the extent of the profile.
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `LeftHanded` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): Sets the turning direction to left handed,
i.e. counter-clockwise when moving along its axis.
- `Midplane` (App::PropertyBool, SketchBased): Extrude symmetric to sketch face
- `Mode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Helix): The helix input mode specifies which properties are set by the user.
Dependent properties are then calculated.
- `Outside` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): If set, the result will be the intersection of the profile and the preexisting body.
- `Pitch` (App::PropertyLength, Helix): The axial distance between two turns.
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `Profile` (App::PropertyLinkSub, SketchBased): Reference to sketch
- `ReferenceAxis` (App::PropertyLinkSub, Helix): The reference axis of the helix.
- `Refine` (App::PropertyBool, Part Design): Refine shape (clean up redundant edges) after adding/subtracting
- `Reversed` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): Determines whether the helix points in the opposite direction of the axis.
- `Shape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `ShapeMaterial` (Materials::PropertyMaterial, )
- `Suppressed` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Is object suppressed
- `SuppressedShape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `Tolerance` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Helix): Fusion Tolerance for the Helix, increase if helical shape does not merge nicely with part.
- `Turns` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Helix): The number of turns in the helix.
- `UpToFace` (App::PropertyLinkSub, SketchBased): Face where feature will end
- `UpToShape` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, SketchBased): Shape where feature will end
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `_Body` (App::PropertyLinkHidden, Base)

### `PartDesign::Helix`
`doc.addObject("PartDesign::Helix", "Name")`

- `AddSubShape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `AllowMultiFace` (App::PropertyBool, SketchBased): Allow multiple faces in profile
- `Angle` (App::PropertyAngle, Helix): The angle of the cone that forms a hull around the helix.
Non-zero values turn the helix into a conical spiral.
Positive values make the radius grow, nevatige shrink.
- `Axis` (App::PropertyVector, Helix): The helix' direction; derived from the reference axis.
- `Base` (App::PropertyVector, Helix): The center point of the helix' start; derived from the reference axis.
- `BaseFeature` (App::PropertyLink, )
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `Growth` (App::PropertyDistance, Helix): The growth of the helix' radius per turn.
Non-zero values turn the helix into a conical spiral.
- `HasBeenEdited` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): If false, the tool will propose an initial value for the pitch based on the profile bounding box,
so that self intersection is avoided.
- `Height` (App::PropertyLength, Helix): The height of the helix' path, not accounting for the extent of the profile.
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `LeftHanded` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): Sets the turning direction to left handed,
i.e. counter-clockwise when moving along its axis.
- `Midplane` (App::PropertyBool, SketchBased): Extrude symmetric to sketch face
- `Mode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Helix): The helix input mode specifies which properties are set by the user.
Dependent properties are then calculated.
- `Outside` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): If set, the result will be the intersection of the profile and the preexisting body.
- `Pitch` (App::PropertyLength, Helix): The axial distance between two turns.
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `Profile` (App::PropertyLinkSub, SketchBased): Reference to sketch
- `ReferenceAxis` (App::PropertyLinkSub, Helix): The reference axis of the helix.
- `Refine` (App::PropertyBool, Part Design): Refine shape (clean up redundant edges) after adding/subtracting
- `Reversed` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): Determines whether the helix points in the opposite direction of the axis.
- `Shape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `ShapeMaterial` (Materials::PropertyMaterial, )
- `Suppressed` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Is object suppressed
- `SuppressedShape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `Tolerance` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Helix): Fusion Tolerance for the Helix, increase if helical shape does not merge nicely with part.
- `Turns` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Helix): The number of turns in the helix.
- `UpToFace` (App::PropertyLinkSub, SketchBased): Face where feature will end
- `UpToShape` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, SketchBased): Shape where feature will end
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `_Body` (App::PropertyLinkHidden, Base)

### `PartDesign::SubShapeBinder`
`doc.addObject("PartDesign::SubShapeBinder", "Name")`

- `BindCopyOnChange` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Base): Disabled: disable copy on change.
Enabled: duplicate properties from binding object that are marked with 'CopyOnChange'.
         Make internal copy of the object with any changed properties to obtain the
         shape of an alternative configuration
Mutated: indicate the binder has already mutated by changing any properties marked with
         'CopyOnChange'. Those properties will not longer be kept in sync between the
         binder and the binding object
- `BindMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Base): Synchronized: auto update binder shape on changed of bound object.
Frozen: disable auto update, but can be updated manually using context menu.
Detached: copy the shape of bound object and then remove the binding immediately.
- `ClaimChildren` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Claim linked object as children
- `Context` (App::PropertyXLink, Base): Stores the context of this binder. It is used for monitoring and auto updating
the relative placement of the bound shape
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `Fuse` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Fuse solids from bound shapes
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `MakeFace` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Create face using wires from bound shapes
- `Offset` (App::PropertyFloat, Offsetting): 2D offset face or wires, 0.0 = no offset
- `OffsetFill` (App::PropertyBool, Offsetting): True = make face between original wire and offset.
- `OffsetIntersection` (App::PropertyBool, Offsetting): False = offset child wires independently.
- `OffsetJoinType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Offsetting): Arcs, Tangent, Intersection
- `OffsetOpenResult` (App::PropertyBool, Offsetting): False = make closed offset from open wire.
- `PartialLoad` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Enable partial loading, which disables auto loading of external document forexternal bound object.
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `Refine` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Refine shape (clean up redundant edges) after adding/subtracting
- `Relative` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Enable relative sub-object binding
- `Shape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `ShapeMaterial` (Materials::PropertyMaterial, )
- `Support` (App::PropertyXLinkSubList, ): Support of the geometry
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `_CopiedLink` (App::PropertyXLinkSub, Base)
- `_Version` (App::PropertyInteger, Base)

### `PartDesign::SubtractiveHelix`
`doc.addObject("PartDesign::SubtractiveHelix", "Name")`

- `AddSubShape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `AllowMultiFace` (App::PropertyBool, SketchBased): Allow multiple faces in profile
- `Angle` (App::PropertyAngle, Helix): The angle of the cone that forms a hull around the helix.
Non-zero values turn the helix into a conical spiral.
Positive values make the radius grow, nevatige shrink.
- `Axis` (App::PropertyVector, Helix): The helix' direction; derived from the reference axis.
- `Base` (App::PropertyVector, Helix): The center point of the helix' start; derived from the reference axis.
- `BaseFeature` (App::PropertyLink, )
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `Growth` (App::PropertyDistance, Helix): The growth of the helix' radius per turn.
Non-zero values turn the helix into a conical spiral.
- `HasBeenEdited` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): If false, the tool will propose an initial value for the pitch based on the profile bounding box,
so that self intersection is avoided.
- `Height` (App::PropertyLength, Helix): The height of the helix' path, not accounting for the extent of the profile.
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `LeftHanded` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): Sets the turning direction to left handed,
i.e. counter-clockwise when moving along its axis.
- `Midplane` (App::PropertyBool, SketchBased): Extrude symmetric to sketch face
- `Mode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Helix): The helix input mode specifies which properties are set by the user.
Dependent properties are then calculated.
- `Outside` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): If set, the result will be the intersection of the profile and the preexisting body.
- `Pitch` (App::PropertyLength, Helix): The axial distance between two turns.
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `Profile` (App::PropertyLinkSub, SketchBased): Reference to sketch
- `ReferenceAxis` (App::PropertyLinkSub, Helix): The reference axis of the helix.
- `Refine` (App::PropertyBool, Part Design): Refine shape (clean up redundant edges) after adding/subtracting
- `Reversed` (App::PropertyBool, Helix): Determines whether the helix points in the opposite direction of the axis.
- `Shape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `ShapeMaterial` (Materials::PropertyMaterial, )
- `Suppressed` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Is object suppressed
- `SuppressedShape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `Tolerance` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Helix): Fusion Tolerance for the Helix, increase if helical shape does not merge nicely with part.
- `Turns` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Helix): The number of turns in the helix.
- `UpToFace` (App::PropertyLinkSub, SketchBased): Face where feature will end
- `UpToShape` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, SketchBased): Shape where feature will end
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `_Body` (App::PropertyLinkHidden, Base)

### `Path::FeatureArea`
`doc.addObject("Path::FeatureArea", "Name")`

- `Accuracy` (App::PropertyPrecision, libarea): Arc fitting accuracy
- `Angle` (App::PropertyAngle, Pocket): Pattern angle in degree
- `AngleShift` (App::PropertyAngle, Pocket): Pattern angle shift for each section
- `CleanDistance` (App::PropertyLength, libarea): Clean polygon smaller than this distance. See https://goo.gl/jox3JY
- `ClipFill` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): ClipperLib clip fill type. 
See https://goo.gl/5pYQQP
- `ClipperScale` (App::PropertyFloat, libarea): ClipperLib operate on integers. This is the scale factor to convert
floating points.
- `Coplanar` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): Specifies the way to check coplanar. 'Force' will discard non coplaner shapes,
but 'Check' only gives warning.
- `Deflection` (App::PropertyPrecision, Area): Deflection for non circular curve discretization. It also also used for
discretizing circular wires when you 'Explode' the shape for wire operations
- `EndType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Offset): ClipperOffset end type. See https://goo.gl/tj7gkX
- `Explode` (App::PropertyBool, Area): If true, Area will explode the first shape into disconnected open edges, 
with all curves discretized, so that later operations like 'Difference' 
behave like wire cutting. Without exploding, 'Difference' in ClipperLib
behave like face cutting.
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `ExtraPass` (App::PropertyInteger, Offset): Number of extra offset pass to generate.
- `Fill` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): Fill the output wires to make a face. 
Auto means make a face if any of the children has a face.
- `FitArcs` (App::PropertyBool, libarea): Enable arc fitting
- `FromCenter` (App::PropertyBool, Pocket): Start pocketing from center
- `JoinType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Offset): ClipperOffset join type. 
See https://goo.gl/4odfQh
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `LastStepover` (App::PropertyLength, Offset): Cutter diameter to step over for the last loop when shrinking with ExtraPass<0, i.e. for
offset pocketing. If =0, use 0.5*Offset.
- `MaxArcPoints` (App::PropertyInteger, libarea): Maximum segments for arc discretization (ignored currently)
- `MinArcPoints` (App::PropertyInteger, libarea): Minimum segments for arc discretization
- `MiterLimit` (App::PropertyFloat, Offset): Miter limit for joint type Miter. See https://goo.gl/K8xX9h
- `Offset` (App::PropertyDistance, Offset): Offset value, positive for expansion, negative for shrinking
- `OpenMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): Specify how to handle open wires. 'None' means combine without openeration.
'Edges' means separate to edges before Union. ClipperLib seems to have an.
urge to close open wires.
- `Operation` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): Boolean operation.
For the first four operations, see https://goo.gl/Gj8RUu.
'Compound' means no operation, normally used to do Area.sortWires().
- `Outline` (App::PropertyBool, Area): Remove all inner wires (holes) before output the final shape
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `PocketExtraOffset` (App::PropertyDistance, Pocket): Extra offset for pocketing
- `PocketLastStepover` (App::PropertyLength, Pocket): Cutter diameter to step over for the last loop when using offset pocket.
If =0, use 0.5*ToolRadius.
- `PocketMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Pocket): Selects the pocket toolpath pattern
- `PocketStepover` (App::PropertyLength, Pocket): Cutter diameter to step over on each pass. If =0, use ToolRadius.
- `Project` (App::PropertyBool, Section): The section is produced by normal projecting the outline
of all added shapes to the section plane, instead of slicing.
- `Reorient` (App::PropertyBool, Area): Re-orient closed wires in wire only shapes so that inner wires become holes.
- `RoundPrecision` (App::PropertyPrecision, Offset): Round joint precision. If =0, it defaults to Accuracy. 
See https://goo.gl/4odfQh
- `SectionCount` (App::PropertyInteger, Section): Number of sections to generate. -1 means full sections.
- `SectionMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Section): Section offset coordinate mode.
'Absolute' means the absolute Z height (given in SectionOffset) to start slicing.
'BoundBox' means relative Z height to the bounding box of all the children shape.
'Workplane' means relative to workplane, minus SectionOffset.
Note that OCC has trouble getting the minimum bounding box of some solids, particularly
those with non-planar surface. It is recommended to use Workplane to specify the intended
starting z height.
- `SectionOffset` (App::PropertyDistance, Section): Offset for the first section. The direction of the offset is
determined by the section direction (i.e. the signess of Stepdown). If going from top down,
a positive value means offset downward, and if bottom up, it means upward
- `SectionTolerance` (App::PropertyPrecision, Section): Offset value added when hitting the boundary.
When the section hits or over the shape boundary, a section with the height of that boundary
will be created. A small offset is usually required to avoid the tangential cut.
- `Shape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `ShapeMaterial` (Materials::PropertyMaterial, )
- `Shift` (App::PropertyDistance, Pocket): Pattern shift distance for each section.
The pocket pattern will be shifted in orthogonal direction by this amount for each section.
This gives a 3D pattern mainly for 3D printing. The shift only applies to 'Offset', 'Grid'
and 'Triangle'
- `Simplify` (App::PropertyBool, libarea): Simplify polygons after operation. See https://goo.gl/Mh9XK1
- `Sources` (App::PropertyLinkList, )
- `Stepdown` (App::PropertyDistance, Section): Step down distance for each section.
Positive value means going from top down, and negative the other way round
- `Stepover` (App::PropertyLength, Offset): Cutter diameter to step over on each pass. If =0, use Offset
- `SubjectFill` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): ClipperLib subject fill type. 
See https://goo.gl/5pYQQP
- `Thicken` (App::PropertyBool, Pocket): Thicken the resulting wires with ToolRadius
- `Tolerance` (App::PropertyPrecision, libarea): Point coincidence tolerance
- `ToolRadius` (App::PropertyLength, Pocket): Tool radius for pocketing
- `Unit` (App::PropertyFloat, libarea): Scaling factor for conversion to inch
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `WorkPlane` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )

### `Path::FeatureAreaPython`
`doc.addObject("Path::FeatureAreaPython", "Name")`

- `Accuracy` (App::PropertyPrecision, libarea): Arc fitting accuracy
- `Angle` (App::PropertyAngle, Pocket): Pattern angle in degree
- `AngleShift` (App::PropertyAngle, Pocket): Pattern angle shift for each section
- `CleanDistance` (App::PropertyLength, libarea): Clean polygon smaller than this distance. See https://goo.gl/jox3JY
- `ClipFill` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): ClipperLib clip fill type. 
See https://goo.gl/5pYQQP
- `ClipperScale` (App::PropertyFloat, libarea): ClipperLib operate on integers. This is the scale factor to convert
floating points.
- `Coplanar` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): Specifies the way to check coplanar. 'Force' will discard non coplaner shapes,
but 'Check' only gives warning.
- `Deflection` (App::PropertyPrecision, Area): Deflection for non circular curve discretization. It also also used for
discretizing circular wires when you 'Explode' the shape for wire operations
- `EndType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Offset): ClipperOffset end type. See https://goo.gl/tj7gkX
- `Explode` (App::PropertyBool, Area): If true, Area will explode the first shape into disconnected open edges, 
with all curves discretized, so that later operations like 'Difference' 
behave like wire cutting. Without exploding, 'Difference' in ClipperLib
behave like face cutting.
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `ExtraPass` (App::PropertyInteger, Offset): Number of extra offset pass to generate.
- `Fill` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): Fill the output wires to make a face. 
Auto means make a face if any of the children has a face.
- `FitArcs` (App::PropertyBool, libarea): Enable arc fitting
- `FromCenter` (App::PropertyBool, Pocket): Start pocketing from center
- `JoinType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Offset): ClipperOffset join type. 
See https://goo.gl/4odfQh
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `LastStepover` (App::PropertyLength, Offset): Cutter diameter to step over for the last loop when shrinking with ExtraPass<0, i.e. for
offset pocketing. If =0, use 0.5*Offset.
- `MaxArcPoints` (App::PropertyInteger, libarea): Maximum segments for arc discretization (ignored currently)
- `MinArcPoints` (App::PropertyInteger, libarea): Minimum segments for arc discretization
- `MiterLimit` (App::PropertyFloat, Offset): Miter limit for joint type Miter. See https://goo.gl/K8xX9h
- `Offset` (App::PropertyDistance, Offset): Offset value, positive for expansion, negative for shrinking
- `OpenMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): Specify how to handle open wires. 'None' means combine without openeration.
'Edges' means separate to edges before Union. ClipperLib seems to have an.
urge to close open wires.
- `Operation` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): Boolean operation.
For the first four operations, see https://goo.gl/Gj8RUu.
'Compound' means no operation, normally used to do Area.sortWires().
- `Outline` (App::PropertyBool, Area): Remove all inner wires (holes) before output the final shape
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `PocketExtraOffset` (App::PropertyDistance, Pocket): Extra offset for pocketing
- `PocketLastStepover` (App::PropertyLength, Pocket): Cutter diameter to step over for the last loop when using offset pocket.
If =0, use 0.5*ToolRadius.
- `PocketMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Pocket): Selects the pocket toolpath pattern
- `PocketStepover` (App::PropertyLength, Pocket): Cutter diameter to step over on each pass. If =0, use ToolRadius.
- `Project` (App::PropertyBool, Section): The section is produced by normal projecting the outline
of all added shapes to the section plane, instead of slicing.
- `Proxy` (App::PropertyPythonObject, )
- `Reorient` (App::PropertyBool, Area): Re-orient closed wires in wire only shapes so that inner wires become holes.
- `RoundPrecision` (App::PropertyPrecision, Offset): Round joint precision. If =0, it defaults to Accuracy. 
See https://goo.gl/4odfQh
- `SectionCount` (App::PropertyInteger, Section): Number of sections to generate. -1 means full sections.
- `SectionMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Section): Section offset coordinate mode.
'Absolute' means the absolute Z height (given in SectionOffset) to start slicing.
'BoundBox' means relative Z height to the bounding box of all the children shape.
'Workplane' means relative to workplane, minus SectionOffset.
Note that OCC has trouble getting the minimum bounding box of some solids, particularly
those with non-planar surface. It is recommended to use Workplane to specify the intended
starting z height.
- `SectionOffset` (App::PropertyDistance, Section): Offset for the first section. The direction of the offset is
determined by the section direction (i.e. the signess of Stepdown). If going from top down,
a positive value means offset downward, and if bottom up, it means upward
- `SectionTolerance` (App::PropertyPrecision, Section): Offset value added when hitting the boundary.
When the section hits or over the shape boundary, a section with the height of that boundary
will be created. A small offset is usually required to avoid the tangential cut.
- `Shape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `ShapeMaterial` (Materials::PropertyMaterial, )
- `Shift` (App::PropertyDistance, Pocket): Pattern shift distance for each section.
The pocket pattern will be shifted in orthogonal direction by this amount for each section.
This gives a 3D pattern mainly for 3D printing. The shift only applies to 'Offset', 'Grid'
and 'Triangle'
- `Simplify` (App::PropertyBool, libarea): Simplify polygons after operation. See https://goo.gl/Mh9XK1
- `Sources` (App::PropertyLinkList, )
- `Stepdown` (App::PropertyDistance, Section): Step down distance for each section.
Positive value means going from top down, and negative the other way round
- `Stepover` (App::PropertyLength, Offset): Cutter diameter to step over on each pass. If =0, use Offset
- `SubjectFill` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Area): ClipperLib subject fill type. 
See https://goo.gl/5pYQQP
- `Thicken` (App::PropertyBool, Pocket): Thicken the resulting wires with ToolRadius
- `Tolerance` (App::PropertyPrecision, libarea): Point coincidence tolerance
- `ToolRadius` (App::PropertyLength, Pocket): Tool radius for pocketing
- `Unit` (App::PropertyFloat, libarea): Scaling factor for conversion to inch
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `WorkPlane` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )

### `Path::FeatureShape`
`doc.addObject("Path::FeatureShape", "Name")`

- `AbsoluteArcCenter` (App::PropertyBool, Path): Use absolute arc center mode (G90.1)
- `ArcPlane` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Path): Arc drawing plane, corresponding to G17, G18, and G19.
If not 'None', the output wires will be transformed to align with the selected plane,
and the corresponding GCode will be inserted.
'Auto' means the plane is determined by the first encountered arc plane. If the found
plane does not align to any GCode plane, XY plane is used.
'Variable' means the arc plane can be changed during operation to align to the
arc encountered.
- `Deflection` (App::PropertyPrecision, Path): Deflection for non circular curve discretization. It also also used for
discretizing circular wires when you 'Explode' the shape for wire operations
- `Direction` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Path): Enforce open path direction
- `EmitPreamble` (App::PropertyBool, Path): Emit preambles
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `FeedRate` (App::PropertyFloat, Path): Normal move feed rate
- `FeedRateVertical` (App::PropertyFloat, Path): Vertical only (step down) move feed rate
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `MinDistance` (App::PropertyLength, Path): minimum distance for the generated new wires. Wires maybe broken if the
algorithm see fits. Set to zero to disable wire breaking.
- `NearestK` (App::PropertyInteger, Path): Nearest k sampling vertices are considered during sorting
- `Orientation` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Path): Enforce loop orientation
'Normal' means CCW for outer wires when looking against the positive axis direction, 
and CW for inner wires. 'Reversed' means the other way round
- `Path` (Path::PropertyPath, Base): The path data of this feature
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `ResumeHeight` (App::PropertyLength, Path): When return from last retraction, this gives the pause height relative to the Z
value of the next move.
- `RetractAxis` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Path): Tool retraction axis
- `RetractThreshold` (App::PropertyLength, Path): If two wire's end points are separated within this threshold, they are consider
as connected. You may want to set this to the tool diameter to keep the tool down.
- `Retraction` (App::PropertyLength, Path): Tool retraction absolute coordinate along retraction axis
- `Segmentation` (App::PropertyLength, Path): Break long curves into segments of this length. One use case is for PCB autolevel,
so that more correction points can be inserted
- `SortAbscissa` (App::PropertyLength, Path): Controls vertex sampling on wire for nearest point searching
The sampling is dong using OCC GCPnts_UniformAbscissa
- `SortMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Path): Wire sorting mode to optimize travel distance.
'2D5' explode shapes into wires, and groups the shapes by its plane. The 'start' position
chooses the first plane to start. The algorithm will then sort within the plane and then
move on to the next nearest plane.
'3D' makes no assumption of planarity. The sorting is done across 3D space.
'Greedy' like '2D5' but will try to minimize travel by searching for nearest path below
the current milling layer. The path in lower layer is only selected if the moving distance
is within the value given in 'threshold'.
- `Sources` (App::PropertyLinkList, )
- `StartPoint` (App::PropertyVector, Path): Feed start position
- `UseStartPoint` (App::PropertyBool, Path): Enable feed start position
- `Verbose` (App::PropertyBool, Path): If true, each motion GCode will contain full coordinate and feedrate
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )

- `AbsoluteArcCenter` (App::PropertyBool, Path): Use absolute arc center mode (G90.1)

- `FeedRate` (App::PropertyFloat, Path): Normal move feed rate
- `FeedRateVertical` (App::PropertyFloat, Path): Vertical only (step down) move feed rate
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `MinDistance` (App::PropertyLength, Path): minimum distance for the generated new wires. Wires maybe broken if the

The sampling is dong using OCC GCPnts_UniformAbscissa
- `SortMode` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Path): Wire sorting mode to optimize travel distance.
'2D5' explode shapes into wires, and groups the shapes by its plane. The 'start' position

'3D' makes no assumption of planarity. The sorting is done across 3D space.

- `Verbose` (App::PropertyBool, Path): If true, each motion GCode will contain full coordinate and feedrate

### `Sketcher::SketchObject`
`doc.addObject("Sketcher::SketchObject", "Name")`

- `ArcFitTolerance` (App::PropertyPrecision, Sketch): Tolerance for fitting arcs of projected external geometry
- `AttacherEngine` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Attachment): Attach engine object driving the attachment.
- `AttacherType` (App::PropertyString, Attachment): Class name of attach engine object driving the attachment.
- `AttachmentOffset` (App::PropertyPlacement, Attachment): Extra placement to apply in addition to attachment (in local coordinates)
- `AttachmentSupport` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, Attachment): Support of the 2D geometry

- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)

### `Surface::Filling`
`doc.addObject("Surface::Filling", "Name")`

- `Anisotropy` (App::PropertyBool, Filling): Anisotropy

- `BoundaryFaces` (App::PropertyStringList, Filling): Boundary Faces
- `BoundaryOrder` (App::PropertyIntegerList, Filling): Order of constraint on boundary faces (C0, G1 and G2 are possible)

- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `MaximumDegree` (App::PropertyInteger, Filling): Maximum curve degree
- `MaximumSegments` (App::PropertyInteger, Filling): Maximum number of segments
- `Placement` (App::PropertyPlacement, )
- `Points` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, Filling): Constraint Points (on Surface)
- `PointsOnCurve` (App::PropertyInteger, Filling): Number of points on an edge for constraint
- `Shape` (Part::PropertyPartShape, )
- `ShapeMaterial` (Materials::PropertyMaterial, )
- `TolAngular` (App::PropertyFloat, Filling): G1 tolerance
- `TolCurvature` (App::PropertyFloat, Filling): G2 tolerance
- `Tolerance2d` (App::PropertyFloat, Filling): 2D Tolerance
- `Tolerance3d` (App::PropertyFloat, Filling): 3D Tolerance
- `UnboundEdges` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, Filling): Unbound constraint edges (C0 is required for edges without a corresponding face)
- `UnboundFaces` (App::PropertyStringList, Filling): Unbound constraint faces
- `UnboundOrder` (App::PropertyIntegerList, Filling): Order of constraint on curve faces (C0, G1 and G2 are possible)
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )

### `TechDraw::DrawBrokenView`
`doc.addObject("TechDraw::DrawBrokenView", "Name")`

- `Breaks` (App::PropertyLinkList, Broken View): Objects in the 3d view that define the start/end points and direction of breaks in this view.
- `Caption` (App::PropertyString, Base): Short text about the view
- `CenterLines` (TechDraw::PropertyCenterLineList, Cosmetics): CenterLine Save/Restore
- `CoarseView` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Coarse View on/off
- `CosmeticEdges` (TechDraw::PropertyCosmeticEdgeList, Cosmetics): CosmeticEdge Save/Restore
- `CosmeticVertexes` (TechDraw::PropertyCosmeticVertexList, Cosmetics): CosmeticVertex Save/Restore
- `Direction` (App::PropertyVector, Projection): Projection Plane normal. The direction you are looking from.
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `Focus` (App::PropertyDistance, Projection): Perspective view focus distance
- `Gap` (App::PropertyLength, Broken View): The separation distance for breaks in this view (unscaled 3d length).
- `GeomFormats` (TechDraw::PropertyGeomFormatList, Cosmetics): Geometry format Save/Restore
- `HardHidden` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Hidden Hard lines
- `IsoCount` (App::PropertyInteger, HLR Parameters): Number of iso parameters lines
- `IsoHidden` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Hidden Iso u, v lines
- `IsoVisible` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Visible Iso u, v lines
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `LockPosition` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Lock View position to parent Page or Group
- `Perspective` (App::PropertyBool, Projection): Perspective(true) or Orthographic(false) projection
- `Rotation` (App::PropertyAngle, Base): Rotation in degrees counterclockwise
- `Scale` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Base): Scale factor of the view. Scale factors like 1:100 can be written as =1/100
- `ScaleType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Base): Scale Type
- `ScrubCount` (App::PropertyInteger, HLR Parameters): The number of times FreeCAD should try to clean the HLR result.
- `SeamHidden` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Hidden Seam lines
- `SeamVisible` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Visible Seam lines
- `SmoothHidden` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Hidden Smooth lines
- `SmoothVisible` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Visible Smooth lines
- `Source` (App::PropertyLinkList, Projection): 3D Shape to view
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `X` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): X position
- `XDirection` (App::PropertyVector, Projection): Projection Plane X Axis in R3. Rotates/Mirrors View
- `XSource` (App::PropertyXLinkList, Projection): External 3D Shape to view
- `Y` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): Y position

- `SeamVisible` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Visible Seam lines

- `UsePreviousCut` (App::PropertyBool, Cut Operation): Use the cut shape from the base view instead of the original object

- `SectionDirection` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Section): Orientation of this Section in the Base View
- `SectionLineStretch` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Appearance): Adjusts the length of the section line.  1.0 is normal length.  1.1 would be 10% longer, 0.9 would be 10% shorter.
- `SectionNormal` (App::PropertyVector, Section): Section Plane normal direction
- `SectionOrigin` (App::PropertyVector, Section): Section Plane Origin
- `SectionSymbol` (App::PropertyString, Section): The identifier for this section
- `SmoothHidden` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Hidden Smooth lines
- `SmoothVisible` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Visible Smooth lines
- `Source` (App::PropertyLinkList, Projection): 3D Shape to view
- `SvgIncluded` (App::PropertyFileIncluded, Cut Surface Format): Embedded Svg hatch file. System use only.
- `TrimAfterCut` (App::PropertyBool, Cut Operation): Trim the resulting shape after the section cut
- `UsePreviousCut` (App::PropertyBool, Cut Operation): Use the cut shape from the base view instead of the original object
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `X` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): X position
- `XDirection` (App::PropertyVector, Projection): Projection Plane X Axis in R3. Rotates/Mirrors View
- `XSource` (App::PropertyXLinkList, Projection): External 3D Shape to view
- `Y` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): Y position

### `TechDraw::DrawViewDimExtent`
`doc.addObject("TechDraw::DrawViewDimExtent", "Name")`

- `AngleOverride` (App::PropertyBool, Override): User specified angles
- `Arbitrary` (App::PropertyBool, Format): Value overridden by user
- `ArbitraryTolerances` (App::PropertyBool, Format): Tolerance values overridden by user
- `BoxCorners` (App::PropertyVectorList, References): Feature bounding box corners as of last reference update.  Used by autocorrect
- `Caption` (App::PropertyString, Base): Short text about the view
- `CosmeticTags` (App::PropertyStringList, ): Id of cosmetic endpoints
- `DirExtent` (App::PropertyInteger, ): Horizontal / Vertical
- `EqualTolerance` (App::PropertyBool, ): If over- and undertolerance are equal
- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `ExtensionAngle` (App::PropertyAngle, Override): Extension line angle
- `FormatSpec` (App::PropertyString, Format): Dimension format
- `FormatSpecOverTolerance` (App::PropertyString, Format): Dimension overtolerance format
- `FormatSpecUnderTolerance` (App::PropertyString, Format): Dimension undertolerance format
- `Inverted` (App::PropertyBool, ): The dimensional value is displayed inverted
- `Label` (App::PropertyString, Base): User name of the object (UTF8)
- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `LineAngle` (App::PropertyAngle, Override): Dimension line angle
- `LockPosition` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Lock View position to parent Page or Group
- `MeasureType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, )
- `OverTolerance` (App::PropertyQuantityConstraint, ): Overtolerance value
If 'Equal Tolerance' is true this is also
the negated value for 'Under Tolerance'
- `References2D` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, ): Projected Geometry References
- `References3D` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, ): 3D Geometry References
- `Rotation` (App::PropertyAngle, Base): Rotation in degrees counterclockwise
- `SavedGeometry` (Part::PropertyTopoShapeList, References): Reference Geometry
- `Scale` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Base): Scale factor of the view. Scale factors like 1:100 can be written as =1/100
- `ScaleType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Base): Scale Type
- `Source` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, ): View containing the  dimension
- `Source3d` (App::PropertyLinkSubList, ): 3d geometry to be dimensioned
- `TheoreticalExact` (App::PropertyBool, ): If theoretical exact (basic) dimension
- `Type` (App::PropertyEnumeration, )
- `UnderTolerance` (App::PropertyQuantityConstraint, ): Undertolerance value
If 'Equal Tolerance' is true it will be replaced
by negative value of 'Over Tolerance'
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `X` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): X position
- `Y` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): Y position

### `TechDraw::DrawViewDraft`
`doc.addObject("TechDraw::DrawViewDraft", "Name")`

- `Caption` (App::PropertyString, Base): Short text about the view

- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)

- `LockPosition` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Lock View position to parent Page or Group
- `OverrideStyle` (App::PropertyBool, Draft view): If True, line color, width and style of this view will override those of rendered objects

- `Symbol` (App::PropertyString, Drawing view): The SVG code defining this symbol

- `Y` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): Y position

- `ExpressionEngine` (App::PropertyExpressionEngine, Base): Property expressions
- `FileGeomPattern` (App::PropertyFile, Cut Surface Format): The PAT pattern file for geometric hatching
- `FileHatchPattern` (App::PropertyFile, Cut Surface Format): The hatch pattern file for the cut surface
- `Focus` (App::PropertyDistance, Projection): Perspective view focus distance
- `FuseBeforeCut` (App::PropertyBool, Cut Operation): Merge Source(s) into a single shape before cutting

- `HatchRotation` (App::PropertyFloat, Cut Surface Format): Rotation of hatch pattern in degrees anti-clockwise
- `HatchScale` (App::PropertyFloat, Cut Surface Format): Hatch pattern size adjustment
- `IsoCount` (App::PropertyInteger, HLR Parameters): Number of iso parameters lines

- `IsoVisible` (App::PropertyBool, HLR Parameters): Show Visible Iso u, v lines

- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)

- `SectionLineStretch` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Appearance): Adjusts the length of the section line.  1.0 is normal length.  1.1 would be 10% longer, 0.9 would be 10% shorter.

- `TrimAfterCut` (App::PropertyBool, Cut Operation): Trim the resulting shape after the section cut
- `UsePreviousCut` (App::PropertyBool, Cut Operation): Use the cut shape from the base view instead of the original object
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `X` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): X position
- `XDirection` (App::PropertyVector, Projection): Projection Plane X Axis in R3. Rotates/Mirrors View

- `Y` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): Y position

- `Label2` (App::PropertyString, Base): User description of the object (UTF8)
- `LineWidth` (App::PropertyFloat, Spreadsheet): The thickness of the cell lines
- `LockPosition` (App::PropertyBool, Base): Lock View position to parent Page or Group
- `Owner` (App::PropertyLink, Drawing view): Feature to which this symbol is attached
- `Rotation` (App::PropertyAngle, Base): Rotation in degrees counterclockwise
- `Scale` (App::PropertyFloatConstraint, Base): Scale factor of the view. Scale factors like 1:100 can be written as =1/100
- `ScaleType` (App::PropertyEnumeration, Base): Scale Type
- `Source` (App::PropertyLink, Spreadsheet): Spreadsheet to view
- `Symbol` (App::PropertyString, Drawing view): The SVG code defining this symbol
- `TextColor` (App::PropertyColor, Spreadsheet): The default color of the text and lines
- `TextSize` (App::PropertyFloat, Spreadsheet): The size of the text
- `Visibility` (App::PropertyBool, )
- `X` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): X position
- `Y` (App::PropertyDistance, Base): Y position
